Theory policy:
 - C6 acceptance is native-structural for degree-7 simple-knot B-spline and equality-constrained for Power/Bezier; floating join/end residuals are diagnostics.
 - All three methods solve exact seventh-derivative energy in square-root form; no Q/Hessian normal-equation solve.
 - QR uses the structurally full-row-rank equality system; no SVD rank truncation or artificial ridge.
 - v/a/j are conservative Bernstein bounds and are measurement only.
 - Geometry certificate is conservative/sufficient finite-direction Bernstein support certification, not exact global distance.
 - Positive cable-tension lower bound is reported and gated separately from geometric separation.
 - Search logs retain one representative failed candidate; every candidate row remains in result for offline analysis.
 - condCZ(coord) and optCoeffCorrection are coordinate-space numerical diagnostics; they must not be interpreted as basis-independent physical quality metrics.

Policy:
 - No HLC/logger/closed-loop simulation is part of planning feasibility.
 - Nominal yaw reference is passed through unchanged.
 - v/a/j are diagnostics only; no empirical derivative limits are introduced.
 - Upstream C6/geometry/tension and nominal actuator feasibility are both required for final pass.
 - Actuator feasibility is sampled in time; it is not claimed as a continuous-time nonlinear actuator certificate.

================================================================================================================================================
 STAGE-G1 AUTOMATIC SYNCHRONIZED REJOIN / VARIABLE-T PARETO CHARACTERIZATION [ONE-CLICK FIRST-LONG AUDIT]
 Fixed avoidance duration S.T: NOT used as the G1 decision.
 Fixed endpoint S.pg:          NOT used as the G1 rejoin endpoint.
 Rejoin definition: D1(T) = nominal_reference_jet(t_trigger + T), matched through derivative order 6.
 Current regression nominal mode: LINEAR constant-velocity reference.
 Candidate anchor: T_clear = nominal passage beyond farthest known oriented-ellipsoid support + system support + d_margin.
 Candidate spacing: tau_obs = traversal time of the largest known obstacle longitudinal diameter.
 Tail landmark indices k: [0 1 2 3], giving T_k = T_clear + k*tau_obs.
 IMPORTANT: k values are characterization landmarks, not safety limits and not the final automatic-T law.
 Acceptance remains C6 + continuous geometry + positive tension + sampled nominal actuator feasibility.
 No weighted quality cost and no arbitrary a/j/swing threshold are introduced.

Theory policy:
 - C6 acceptance is native-structural for degree-7 simple-knot B-spline and equality-constrained for Power/Bezier; floating join/end residuals are diagnostics.
 - All three methods solve exact seventh-derivative energy in square-root form; no Q/Hessian normal-equation solve.
 - QR uses the structurally full-row-rank equality system; no SVD rank truncation or artificial ridge.
 - v/a/j are conservative Bernstein bounds and are measurement only.
 - Geometry certificate is conservative/sufficient finite-direction Bernstein support certification, not exact global distance.
 - Positive cable-tension lower bound is reported and gated separately from geometric separation.
 - Search logs retain one representative failed candidate; every candidate row remains in result for offline analysis.
 - condCZ(coord) and optCoeffCorrection are coordinate-space numerical diagnostics; they must not be interpreted as basis-independent physical quality metrics.

Theory policy:
 - C6 acceptance is native-structural for degree-7 simple-knot B-spline and equality-constrained for Power/Bezier; floating join/end residuals are diagnostics.
 - All three methods solve exact seventh-derivative energy in square-root form; no Q/Hessian normal-equation solve.
 - QR uses the structurally full-row-rank equality system; no SVD rank truncation or artificial ridge.
 - v/a/j are conservative Bernstein bounds and are measurement only.
 - Geometry certificate is conservative/sufficient finite-direction Bernstein support certification, not exact global distance.
 - Positive cable-tension lower bound is reported and gated separately from geometric separation.
 - Search logs retain one representative failed candidate; every candidate row remains in result for offline analysis.
 - condCZ(coord) and optCoeffCorrection are coordinate-space numerical diagnostics; they must not be interpreted as basis-independent physical quality metrics.

Policy:
 - No HLC/logger/closed-loop simulation is part of planning feasibility.
 - Nominal yaw reference is passed through unchanged.
 - v/a/j are diagnostics only; no empirical derivative limits are introduced.
 - Upstream C6/geometry/tension and nominal actuator feasibility are both required for final pass.
 - Actuator feasibility is sampled in time; it is not claimed as a continuous-time nonlinear actuator certificate.

 STAGE-G1 AUTOMATIC SYNCHRONIZED REJOIN / VARIABLE-T PARETO CHARACTERIZATION [ONE-CLICK V3 PERSISTENT AUDIT]
 Fixed avoidance duration S.T: NOT used as the G1 decision.
 Fixed endpoint S.pg:          NOT used as the G1 rejoin endpoint.
 Rejoin definition: D1(T) = nominal_reference_jet(t_trigger + T), matched through derivative order 6.
 Current regression nominal mode: LINEAR constant-velocity reference.
 Candidate anchor: T_clear = nominal passage beyond farthest known oriented-ellipsoid support + system support + d_margin.
 Candidate spacing: tau_obs = traversal time of the largest known obstacle longitudinal diameter.
 Tail landmark indices k: [0 1 2 3], giving T_k = T_clear + k*tau_obs.
 IMPORTANT: k values are characterization landmarks, not safety limits and not the final automatic-T law.
 Acceptance remains C6 + continuous geometry + positive tension + sampled nominal actuator feasibility.
 No weighted quality cost and no arbitrary a/j/swing threshold are introduced.

Theory policy:
 - C6 acceptance is native-structural for degree-7 simple-knot B-spline and equality-constrained for Power/Bezier; floating join/end residuals are diagnostics.
 - All three methods solve exact seventh-derivative energy in square-root form; no Q/Hessian normal-equation solve.
 - QR uses the structurally full-row-rank equality system; no SVD rank truncation or artificial ridge.
 - v/a/j are conservative Bernstein bounds and are measurement only.
 - Geometry certificate is conservative/sufficient finite-direction Bernstein support certification, not exact global distance.
 - Positive cable-tension lower bound is reported and gated separately from geometric separation.
 - Search logs retain one representative failed candidate; every candidate row remains in result for offline analysis.
 - condCZ(coord) and optCoeffCorrection are coordinate-space numerical diagnostics; they must not be interpreted as basis-independent physical quality metrics.

 - No HLC/logger/closed-loop simulation is part of planning feasibility.
 - Nominal yaw reference is passed through unchanged.

 STAGE-G1 AUTOMATIC SYNCHRONIZED REJOIN / VARIABLE-T PARETO CHARACTERIZATION [BS STATIC CACHE PHASE-1]
 Fixed avoidance duration S.T: NOT used as the G1 decision.
 Fixed endpoint S.pg:          NOT used as the G1 rejoin endpoint.
 Rejoin definition: D1(T) = nominal_reference_jet(t_trigger + T), matched through derivative order 6.
 Current regression nominal mode: LINEAR constant-velocity reference.
 Candidate anchor: T_clear = nominal passage beyond farthest known oriented-ellipsoid support + system support + d_margin.
 Candidate spacing: tau_obs = traversal time of the largest known obstacle longitudinal diameter.
 Tail landmark indices k: [0 1 2 3], giving T_k = T_clear + k*tau_obs.
 IMPORTANT: k values are characterization landmarks, not safety limits and not the final automatic-T law.
 Acceptance remains C6 + continuous geometry + positive tension + sampled nominal actuator feasibility.
 No weighted quality cost and no arbitrary a/j/swing threshold are introduced.

Theory policy:
 - C6 acceptance is native-structural for degree-7 simple-knot B-spline and equality-constrained for Power/Bezier; floating join/end residuals are diagnostics.
 - All three methods solve exact seventh-derivative energy in square-root form; no Q/Hessian normal-equation solve.
 - QR uses the structurally full-row-rank equality system; no SVD rank truncation or artificial ridge.
 - v/a/j are conservative Bernstein bounds and are measurement only.
 - Geometry certificate is conservative/sufficient finite-direction Bernstein support certification, not exact global distance.
 - Positive cable-tension lower bound is reported and gated separately from geometric separation.
 - Search logs retain one representative failed candidate; every candidate row remains in result for offline analysis.
 - condCZ(coord) and optCoeffCorrection are coordinate-space numerical diagnostics; they must not be interpreted as basis-independent physical quality metrics.

Policy:
 - No HLC/logger/closed-loop simulation is part of planning feasibility.
 - Nominal yaw reference is passed through unchanged.
 - v/a/j are diagnostics only; no empirical derivative limits are introduced.
 - Upstream C6/geometry/tension and nominal actuator feasibility are both required for final pass.
 - Actuator feasibility is sampled in time; it is not claimed as a continuous-time nonlinear actuator certificate.
================================================================================================================================================


================================================================================================================================================
 STAGE-G1 AUTOMATIC SYNCHRONIZED REJOIN / VARIABLE-T PARETO CHARACTERIZATION [BS CACHE PHASE-2]
 Fixed avoidance duration S.T: NOT used as the G1 decision.
 Fixed endpoint S.pg:          NOT used as the G1 rejoin endpoint.
 Rejoin definition: D1(T) = nominal_reference_jet(t_trigger + T), matched through derivative order 6.
 Current regression nominal mode: LINEAR constant-velocity reference.
 Candidate anchor: T_clear = nominal passage beyond farthest known oriented-ellipsoid support + system support + d_margin.
 Candidate spacing: tau_obs = traversal time of the largest known obstacle longitudinal diameter.
 Tail landmark indices k: [0 1 2 3], giving T_k = T_clear + k*tau_obs.
 IMPORTANT: k values are characterization landmarks, not safety limits and not the final automatic-T law.
 Acceptance remains C6 + continuous geometry + positive tension + sampled nominal actuator feasibility.
 No weighted quality cost and no arbitrary a/j/swing threshold are introduced.
================================================================================================================================================

Theory policy:
 - C6 acceptance is native-structural for degree-7 simple-knot B-spline and equality-constrained for Power/Bezier; floating join/end residuals are diagnostics.
 - All three methods solve exact seventh-derivative energy in square-root form; no Q/Hessian normal-equation solve.
 - QR uses the structurally full-row-rank equality system; no SVD rank truncation or artificial ridge.
 - v/a/j are conservative Bernstein bounds and are measurement only.
 - Geometry certificate is conservative/sufficient finite-direction Bernstein support certification, not exact global distance.
 - Positive cable-tension lower bound is reported and gated separately from geometric separation.
 - Search logs retain one representative failed candidate; every candidate row remains in result for offline analysis.
 - condCZ(coord) and optCoeffCorrection are coordinate-space numerical diagnostics; they must not be interpreted as basis-independent physical quality metrics.

Policy:
 - No HLC/logger/closed-loop simulation is part of planning feasibility.
 - Nominal yaw reference is passed through unchanged.
 - v/a/j are diagnostics only; no empirical derivative limits are introduced.
 - Upstream C6/geometry/tension and nominal actuator feasibility are both required for final pass.
 - Actuator feasibility is sampled in time; it is not claimed as a continuous-time nonlinear actuator certificate.

 STAGE-G1 AUTOMATIC SYNCHRONIZED REJOIN / VARIABLE-T PARETO CHARACTERIZATION [G1-SCOPED GEOMETRY-FIRST FAST-PATH]
 Fixed avoidance duration S.T: NOT used as the G1 decision.
 Fixed endpoint S.pg:          NOT used as the G1 rejoin endpoint.
 Rejoin definition: D1(T) = nominal_reference_jet(t_trigger + T), matched through derivative order 6.
 Current regression nominal mode: LINEAR constant-velocity reference.
 Candidate anchor: T_clear = nominal passage beyond farthest known oriented-ellipsoid support + system support + d_margin.
 Candidate spacing: tau_obs = traversal time of the largest known obstacle longitudinal diameter.
 Tail landmark indices k: [0 1 2 3], giving T_k = T_clear + k*tau_obs.
 IMPORTANT: k values are characterization landmarks, not safety limits and not the final automatic-T law.
 Acceptance remains C6 + continuous geometry + positive tension + sampled nominal actuator feasibility.
 No weighted quality cost and no arbitrary a/j/swing threshold are introduced.
